- **splice-graph-path-search-implementation**: Use when you have a labeled splice graph built from integrated mappings of multiple aligners (labels 1, 2+, 2-) and need to extract transcript-representing paths, particularly to recover lowly expressed transcripts. - **splice-graph-transcript-assembly**: Use when RNase R-treated paired-end RNA-seq data have back-spliced junctions detected by CIRCexplorer2, CIRI2, or UROBORUS and you need full-length circRNA transcript sequences with FPKM/TPM isoform-level quantification. - **splice-issue**: Break a large GitHub issue from bbvch-ai/aihub-core into self-contained, independently mergeable sub-issues with blocked-by dependency relationships. Takes an issue number or URL as argument. Do NOT use for implementation planning within a single issue (use /plan-issue), creating PRs (use /create-pr), or fetching issue details without splicing (use gh issue view). - **splice-issue**: Break a large GitHub issue into independent, mergeable sub-issues with clear scope and acceptance criteria. Do NOT use for planning implementation steps within a single issue (use plan-issue) or for creating a fresh issue from scratch. Use for /splice-pr, "split this PR", "break this branch up", "this PR is too big to ship in one go", "stack this", "can we land this in pieces", or when a PR mixes a migration, a refactor and a behaviour change and deploying it all at once feels risky, even if the user doesn't say "splice". - **splice-production-formats**: Production craft for a finished video with Splice by film.fun: the segment anatomy of a social short, advert, explainer, news show, product demo or music video, the Splice asset each beat needs (image, video, voiceover, music, Motion composition, captions), durations for a target length, assembly notes and a pre-render QA checklist. - **splice-production-stages**: Walk a Splice production from an idea to a finished cut, stage by stage: script, storyboard, shotlist, shoot (takes of every shot, AI or live action), Studio cut and export. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/splicing-based-velocity-baseline-benchmarking))
